                async scan() {\n
                    const file=this.$refs.file.files[0]; if(!file){this.message='Choose a driving licence image first.';return;}\n
                    this.busy=true;this.message='Scanning licence…';const body=new FormData();body.append('document',file);body.append('expected_document_type','DRIVING_LICENCE');\n

                    try { const response=await fetch('http://localhost/driver/vehicles/documents/ocr',{method:'POST',headers:{'Accept':'application/json','X-CSRF-TOKEN':'8sREAmtGn2hQvpgNKZiD6XIbfGi8fjnxgQQa1Cji'},body,signal:controller.signal});const data=await response.json();if(!response.ok)throw new Error(data.message||'Unable to scan the licence.');const fields=data.fields||{};const set=(name,key,date=false)=>{const value=fields[key]?.value;if(value)this.$refs[name].value=date?this.dateValue(value):value};set('holder','name');set('identity','identity_no');set('class','licence_class');set('from','valid_from',true);set('until','valid_until',true);this.licenceClasses=this.parseLicenceClasses(this.$refs.class.value);this.scanned=true;this.message='Scan complete. Extracted details are locked and cannot be edited.'; }\n
                    catch(error){this.scanned=false;this.message=error.name==='AbortError'?'Scanning took too long. Please retry with a smaller, clearer image.':error.message;} finally{clearTimeout(timeout);this.busy=false;}\n

    <script>\n
        window.rideSearchForm = (endpoint, destinationText, destinationPlaceId) => ({\n
            timer: null,\n
            destination: { text: destinationText, placeId: destinationPlaceId, suggestions: [], open: false },\n
            async search() {\n
                this.destination.placeId = '';\n
                this.destination.suggestions = [];\n
                this.destination.open = true;\n
\n
                clearTimeout(this.timer);\n
                if (this.destination.text.trim().length < 2) {\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
\n
                this.timer = setTimeout(async () => {\n
                    try {\n
                        const response = await window.axios.get(endpoint, { params: { input: this.destination.text.trim() } });\n
                        this.destination.suggestions = response.data.data || [];\n
                    } catch (_) {\n
                        this.destination.suggestions = [];\n
                    }\n
                }, 250);\n
            },\n
            select(suggestion) {\n
                this.destination.text = suggestion.text;\n
                this.destination.placeId = suggestion.place_id;\n
                this.destination.suggestions = [];\n
                this.destination.open = false;\n
            },\n
        });\n
    </script>\n
                            </main>\n
        </div>\n
        <div x-data="faqBot({ featuredEndpoint: 'http:\/\/localhost\/faq-bot\/featured', answerEndpoint: 'http:\/\/localhost\/faq-bot\/answer' })" class="fixed bottom-24 right-5 z-[90] [font-family:Inter,sans-serif] sm:bottom-24">\n
    <button\n
        type="button"\n
        x-show="!open"\n
        x-transition\n
        @click="open = true; if (!featured.length) loadFeatured(); $nextTick(() => $refs.question.focus())"\n
        @open-faq-bot.window="openBot($event)"\n

>\n
